This is essential for normalizing manufacturing defect logs where engineers use informal language.\n\n## Key Libraries\n\n### 1. RapidFuzz (Recommended)\nFast string matching library with multiple algorithms.\n\n**Installation:**\n```bash\npip install rapidfuzz\n```\n\n**Key Functions:**\n\n#### `fuzz.ratio()` - Simple similarity (0-100)\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"fixture contact\", \"fixture 接触不良\")\n# Returns: similarity score 0-100\n```\n\n#### `fuzz.partial_ratio()` - Substring matching\n```python\nscore = fuzz.partial_ratio(\"open\", \"R152_NET open, suspect cold solder\")\n# Good for finding keywords in longer text\n```\n\n#### `fuzz.token_sort_ratio()` - Order-insensitive\n```python\nscore = fuzz.token_sort_ratio(\"cold solder U202\", \"U202 虚焊\")\n# Matches regardless of word order\n```\n\n#### `process.extractOne()` - Find best match from list\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import process, fuzz\n\nquery = \"治具/探针接触不良\"\nchoices = [\"Fixture contact unstable\", \"Cold solder suspected\", \"Open circuit\"]\nresult = process.extractOne(query, choices, scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio)\n# Returns: (best_match, score, index)\n```\n\n#### `process.extract()` - Find top N matches\n```python\nresults = process.extract(query, choices, limit=3, scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio)\n# Returns: [(match, score, index), ...]\n```\n\n### 2. difflib (Standard Library)\nPython's built-in string matching module.\n\n```python\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\ndef similarity(a, b):\n    return SequenceMatcher(None, a, b).ratio()\n\nscore = similarity(\"open circuit\", \"开路\")\n```\n\n## Common Patterns for Manufacturing Defect Matching\n\n### Pattern 1: Keyword-based matching\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\ndef match_by_keywords(text, codebook_keywords):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match text against keyword examples from codebook\n    codebook_keywords: \"open, 开路, C87, VDD_5V\"\n    \"\"\"\n    keywords = [k.strip() for k in codebook_keywords.split(',')]\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n\n    matches = []\n    for keyword in keywords:\n        if keyword.lower() in text_lower:\n            matches.append(keyword)\n\n    return len(matches) > 0, matches\n```\n\n### Pattern 2: Component extraction + matching\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract component references like U202, R152, C87\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b[A-Z]+\\d+\\b'\n    return re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n\ndef extract_net_names(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract net names like VDD_5V, SW_NODE\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b'\n    candidates = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n    # Filter out common abbreviations\n    return [c for c in candidates if len(c) > 2 and '_' in c]\n```\n\n### Pattern 3: Multi-language matching\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\ndef multilingual_match(text, standard_label, keywords_examples):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match considering both English and Chinese content\n    \"\"\"\n    # Combine label and keywords for comprehensive matching\n    reference_text = f\"{standard_label} {keywords_examples}\"\n\n    # Use token-based matching (word order independent)\n    score = fuzz.token_sort_ratio(text.lower(), reference_text.lower())\n\n    return score\n```\n\n### Pattern 4: Station-aware filtering\n```python\ndef filter_by_station(codebook_df, station):\n    \"\"\"\n    Filter codebook entries applicable to the test station\n    \"\"\"\n    mask = codebook_df['station_scope'].str.contains(station, case=False, na=False)\n    return codebook_df[mask]\n```\n\n## Complete Matching Pipeline\n\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz, process\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef find_best_match(raw_text, codebook_df, station, threshold=60):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching code from codebook\n\n    Parameters:\n    - raw_text: Engineer's raw input text\n    - codebook_df: DataFrame with columns [code, standard_label, keywords_examples, station_scope]\n    - station: Test station (ICT, FCT, etc.)\n    - threshold: Minimum confidence score (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n    - (code, label, confidence) or (None, None, 0)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter by station\n    station_filtered = filter_by_station(codebook_df, station)\n\n    if station_filtered.empty:\n        return None, None, 0.0\n\n    # Prepare search corpus (combine label + keywords)\n    search_corpus = (station_filtered['standard_label'] + ' ' +\n                     station_filtered['keywords_examples']).tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        raw_text,\n        search_corpus,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        match_text, score, idx = result\n        matched_row = station_filtered.iloc[idx]\n        confidence = score / 100.0  # Normalize to 0-1\n        return matched_row['code'], matched_row['standard_label'], confidence\n\n    return None, None, 0.0\n```\n\n## Confidence Calibration\n\nFor engineering use, calibrate confidence scores:\n\n```python\ndef calibrate_confidence(raw_score, match_quality_factors):\n    \"\"\"\n    Adjust confidence based on match quality\n\n    raw_score: 0-100 from fuzzy matcher\n    match_quality_factors: dict with boolean flags\n    \"\"\"\n    confidence = raw_score / 100.0\n\n    # Boost confidence if exact component match\n    if match_quality_factors.get('component_match'):\n        confidence = min(1.0, confidence + 0.1)\n\n    # Boost if station matches\n    if match_quality_factors.get('station_match'):\n        confidence = min(1.0, confidence + 0.05)\n\n    # Reduce if no keyword overlap\n    if not match_quality_factors.get('keyword_overlap'):\n        confidence *= 0.8\n\n    return round(confidence, 2)\n```\n\n## Setting UNKNOWN threshold\n\n```python\nUNKNOWN_THRESHOLD = 0.6  # Below 60% confidence -> UNKNOWN\n\ndef normalize_with_unknown(text, codebook_df, station):\n    code, label, confidence = find_best_match(text, codebook_df, station, threshold=50)\n\n    if confidence < UNKNOWN_THRESHOLD:\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", max(0.0, confidence - 0.2)  # Lower confidence for UNKNOWN\n\n    return code, label, confidence\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always preprocess**: Lowercase, strip whitespace\n2. **Use token-based scorers**: Better for word order variations\n3. **Filter by context**: Station, product type before matching\n4. **Combine methods**: Keyword + fuzzy matching\n5. **Handle multilingual**: Chinese-English mixed text\n6. **Set appropriate thresholds**: Too low = This guide covers Python's json module and best practices for generating structured manufacturing data.\n\n## Basic JSON Operations\n\n### Import\n```python\nimport json\n```\n\n### Writing JSON\n\n#### Basic write to file\n```python\ndata = {\"name\": \"value\", \"items\": [1, 2, 3]}\n\n# Write to file\nwith open('output.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n```\n\n#### Convert to JSON string\n```python\njson_string = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n```\n\n### Reading JSON\n\n```python\n# Read from file\nwith open('input.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# Parse from string\ndata = json.loads(json_string)\n```\n\n## Important Parameters\n\n### `ensure_ascii=False`\n**Critical for Chinese/Unicode characters**\n```python\n# Without ensure_ascii=False\njson.dumps({\"text\": \"开路\"})\n# Output: '{\"text\": \"\\\\u5f00\\\\u8def\"}'\n\n# With ensure_ascii=False\njson.dumps({\"text\": \"开路\"}, ensure_ascii=False)\n# Output: '{\"text\": \"开路\"}'\n```\n\n### `indent`\n**For readable output**\n```python\n# Compact (no indent)\njson.dumps(data)\n# '{\"name\":\"value\",\"items\":[1,2,3]}'\n\n# Pretty printed (indent=2)\njson.dumps(data, indent=2)\n# {\n#   \"name\": \"value\",\n#   \"items\": [\n#     1,\n#     2,\n#     3\n#   ]\n# }\n```\n\n## Building Nested Structures\n\n### Manufacturing Test Record Format\n\n```python\ndef create_segment(segment_id, span_text, pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale):\n    \"\"\"Create a normalized segment\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n        \"span_text\": span_text,\n        \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n        \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n        \"confidence\": round(confidence, 2),  # Check required fields\n        required = [\"record_id\", \"product_id\", \"station\", \"engineer_id\", \"raw_reason_text\", \"normalized\"]\n        for field in required:\n            assert field in record, f\"Missing field: {field}\"\n\n        # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01P5eYnZjGh85TxnQXVBQbns","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_016pCo71JwXPWzWx5gbGm4QG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/environment/skills/text-segmentation.md","content":"# Text Segmentation for Multi-Issue Defect Reports\n\n## Overview\nManufacturing defect reports often contain multiple issues in a single text field, separated by delimiters or connectives. Proper segmentation is critical before matching each segment to codebook entries.\n\n## Common Delimiters in Manufacturing Logs\n\n### Explicit Delimiters\n- `;` - Semicolon\n- `、` - Chinese enumeration comma\n- `&` - Ampersand\n- `+` - Plus sign\n- `/` - Forward slash (sometimes)\n- `|` - Pipe\n\n### Connective Phrases\n- `and` / `且` / `并且`\n- `also` / `另` / `还有`\n- `or` / `或` / `或者`\n\n### Example Texts with Multiple Issues\n```\n\"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK 浮高 & 静态电流偏高，疑似短路趋势/器件异常\"\n\n\"SPI_MISO open, suspect cold solder + probe worn causing bad contact, replace pin\"\n\n\"I2C_SDA 开路，疑似虚焊/少锡；Station=ICT, FailCode=E330，建议复测确认\"\n```\n\n## Segmentation Strategies\n\n### Strategy 1: Simple Delimiter Splitting\n\n```python\ndef simple_segment(text):\n    \"\"\"Split by common delimiters\"\"\"\n    delimiters = [';', '、', '&', '+']\n\n    segments = [text]\n    for delimiter in delimiters:\n        new_segments = []\n        for seg in segments:\n            new_segments.extend(seg.split(delimiter))\n        segments = new_segments\n\n    # Clean and filter\n    segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n    return segments\n```\n\n### Strategy 2: Multi-Character Delimiter Splitting\n\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef regex_segment(text):\n    \"\"\"Split by multiple delimiters using regex\"\"\"\n    # Split by ; & + 、 and connective phrases\n    pattern = r'[;;&+、]|(?:\\s+and\\s+)|(?:\\s+或\\s+)|(?:\\s+并且\\s+)'\n    segments = re.split(pattern, text)\n\n    # Clean whitespace and empty strings\n    segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n    return segments\n```\n\n### Strategy 3: Contextual Segmentation\n\n```python\ndef contextual_segment(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment based on manufacturing context patterns\n    Looks for issue indicators that signal new segments\n    \"\"\"\n    # Indicators of new issues\n    indicators = [\n        r'(?:^|\\s)(?:suspect|疑似|可能)',  # Suspicion words\n        r'(?:^|\\s)(?:also|另|还有|并且)',   # Additional issue words\n        r'(?:^|\\s)(?:and|且)',              # Conjunction\n    ]\n\n    # Try to split at indicators\n    for pattern in indicators:\n        if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n            # More sophisticated splitting logic here\n            pass\n\n    # Fallback to simple delimiter split\n    return regex_segment(text)\n```\n\n### Strategy 4: Slash Handling (Special Case)\n\nSlashes can mean \"or\" (multiple descriptions of same issue) or separate issues:\n\n```python\ndef smart_slash_handling(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Handle slashes intelligently\n    - \"虚焊/浮高\" = cold solder OR floating (same issue, synonyms)\n    - \"issue1 / issue2\" = separate issues if contextually different\n    \"\"\"\n    # Split by major delimiters first\n    major_segments = regex_segment(text)\n\n    final_segments = []\n    for seg in major_segments:\n        # Check if slash separates synonyms or separate issues\n        if '/' in seg:\n            parts = seg.split('/')\n\n            # If parts are short and similar (likely synonyms), keep together\n            if len(parts) == 2 and len(parts[0]) < 20 and len(parts[1]) < 20:\n                # Keep as one segment (synonyms)\n                final_segments.append(seg)\n            else:\n                # Split as separate issues\n                final_segments.extend([p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()])\n        else:\n            final_segments.append(seg)\n\n    return final_segments\n```\n\n## Handling Nested Information\n\nSome texts contain issue + context (not separate issues):\n\n```python\ndef extract_primary_issues(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract primary issues while preserving context\n\n    Example:\n    \"I2C_SDA 开路，疑似虚焊/少锡；Station=ICT\"\n    -> Primary issue: \"I2C_SDA 开路，疑似虚焊/少锡\"\n    -> Metadata: \"Station=ICT\" (keep with main issue)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove metadata patterns that aren't actual issues\n    metadata_patterns = [\n        r'Station\\s*=\\s*\\w+',\n        r'FailCode\\s*=\\s*\\w+',\n        r'建议\\w+确认',\n        r'需确认\\w*',\n    ]\n\n    cleaned = text\n    for pattern in metadata_patterns:\n        cleaned = re.sub(pattern, '', cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    # Now segment the cleaned text\n    return regex_segment(cleaned)\n```\n\n## Complete Segmentation Pipeline\n\n```python\nimport re\n\nclass TextSegmenter:\n    \"\"\"Complete text segmentation for manufacturing defect reports\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.major_delimiters = r'[;;&、]'\n        self.minor_delimiters = r'[+]'\n        self.connectives = r'(?:\\s+and\\s+)|(?:\\s+或\\s+)|(?:\\s+并且\\s+)|(?:\\s+also\\s+)'\n\n    def segment(self, text):\n        \"\"\"Main segmentation method\"\"\"\n        if not text or not text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Remove metadata\n        text = self._remove_metadata(text)\n\n        # Split by major delimiters\n        segments = self._split_by_delimiters(text)\n\n        # Clean and validate\n        segments = self._clean_segments(segments)\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _remove_metadata(self, text):\n        \"\"\"Remove non-issue metadata\"\"\"\n        patterns = [\n            r'Station\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n            r'FailCode\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n        ]\n\n        cleaned = text\n        for pattern in patterns:\n            cleaned = re.sub(pattern, '', cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        return cleaned.strip()\n\n    def _split_by_delimiters(self, text):\n        \"\"\"Split by all delimiter types\"\"\"\n        # Combine all delimiter patterns\n        pattern = f'({self.major_delimiters}|{self.connectives})'\n        segments = re.split(pattern, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        # Filter out the delimiter matches themselves\n        segments = [s for s in segments if not re.match(pattern, s, re.IGNORECASE)]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _clean_segments(self, segments):\n        \"\"\"Clean, filter and deduplicate segments\"\"\"\n        cleaned = []\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Strip whitespace\n            seg = seg.strip()\n            seg = seg.strip(',，.。')  # Remove trailing punctuation\n\n            # Skip empty or too short\n            if not seg or len(seg) < 3:\n                continue\n\n            # Skip pure punctuation or numbers\n            if re.match(r'^[\\W\\d]+$', seg):\n                continue\n\n            cleaned.append(seg)\n\n        # Remove duplicates while preserving order\n        seen = set()\n        unique = []\n        for seg in cleaned:\n            if seg.lower() not in seen:\n                seen.add(seg.lower())\n                unique.append(seg)\n\n        return unique\n\n# Usage\nsegmenter = TextSegmenter()\nsegments = segmenter.segment(\"治具/探针接触不良 & 静态电流偏高，疑似短路\")\n# Returns: [\"治具/探针接触不良\", \"静态电流偏高，疑似短路\"]\n```\n\n## Creating Segment IDs\n\n```python\ndef create_segment_id(record_id, segment_index):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create segment ID in format: <record_id>-S<i>\n\n    Example: \"R0000001\" + 1 -> \"R0000001-S1\"\n    \"\"\"\n    return f\"{record_id}-S{segment_index}\"\n\n# Usage in processing\ndef process_record(record_id, raw_text):\n    segmenter = TextSegmenter()\n    segments = segmenter.segment(raw_text)\n\n    segment_results = []\n    for i, seg_text in enumerate(segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = create_segment_id(record_id, i)\n\n        # Process this segment...\n        result = {\n            \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n            \"span_text\": seg_text,\n            # ... other fields\n        }\n        segment_results.append(result)\n\n    return segment_results\n```\n\n## Edge Cases\n\n### Single Issue (No Segmentation Needed)\n```python\ntext = \"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK\"\nsegments = reseat sometimes passes\"\nsegments = segmenter.segment(text)\n# Returns: [\n#   \"idle current high, suspect partial short/component issue 重测过\",\n#   \"fixture contact unstable, reseat sometimes passes\"\n# ]\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Preserve context** - Keep descriptive parts with main issue\n2. **Handle synonyms** - \"虚焊/浮高\" should stay together\n3. **Remove metadata** - Filter ]\n\n    for text, expected_count in test_cases:\n        segments = segmenter.segment(text)\n        assert len(segments) == expected_count, f\"Failed: {text}\"\n\n    print(\"All segmentation tests Regular Expression Pattern Matching\n\n## Overview\nRegular expressions (regex) are essential for extracting structured information from unstructured manufacturing defect text, such as component names, net names, and keywords.\n\n## Python re Module\n\n```python\nimport re\n```\n\n## Basic Pattern Matching\n\n### Search for pattern\n```python\n# Find first match\nmatch = re.search(r'pattern', text)\nif match:\n    found_text = match.group()\n\n# Find all matches\nmatches = re.findall(r'pattern', text)\n# Returns: list of matched strings\n```\n\n### Match at start\n```python\n# Check if text starts with pattern\nmatch = re.match(r'^pattern', text)\n```\n\n### Replace patterns\n```python\n# Replace all occurrences\nnew_text = re.sub(r'pattern', 'replacement', text)\n\n# Replace with function\ndef replacer(match):\n    return match.group().upper()\n\nnew_text = re.sub(r'pattern', replacer, text)\n```\n\n### Split by pattern\n```python\n# Split text by delimiter pattern\nparts = re.split(r'[;,&]', text)\n```\n\n## Common Patterns for Manufacturing Data\n\n### Component Names\n```python\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract component references: U202, R152, C87, Q15, etc.\n    Format: Letter(s) + Number(s)\n    \"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b'\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n    return matches\n\n# Examples:\n# \"U202 虚焊\" -> [\"U202\"]\n# \"R152_NET open\" -> [\"R152\"]\n# \"C87 and Q15\" -> [\"C87\", \"Q15\"]\n```\n\n### Net Names / Signal Names\n```python\ndef extract_net_names(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract net/signal names: VDD_5V, SW_NODE, I2C_SDA, etc.\n    Format: CAPS with underscores\n    \"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,})\\b'\n    candidates = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n\n    # Filter out common abbreviations that aren't net names\n    exclusions = {'OK', 'NG', 'ICT', 'FCT', 'BST', 'ESD'}\n    net_names = [c for c in candidates if c not in exclusions and '_' in c]\n\n    return net_names\n\n# Examples:\n# \"SW_NODE open\" -> [\"SW_NODE\"]\n# \"I2C_SDA 开路\" -> [\"I2C_SDA\"]\n# \"VDD_5V and GND\" -> [\"VDD_5V\", \"GND\"]\n```\n\n### Test Station Names\n```python\ndef extract_station(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract test station names\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b(ICT|FCT|BST|FUNC|FLASH|BURNIN|HIPOT|RF_CAL)\\b'\n    match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    return match.group().upper() if match else None\n\n# Examples:\n# \"Station=ICT\" -> \"ICT\"\n# \"at FCT station\" -> \"FCT\"\n```\n\n### Error/Fail Codes\n```python\ndef extract_fail_codes(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract fail codes: E101, E702, F001, S021, B090, etc.\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d{3,4})\\b'\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n    return matches\n\n# Examples:\n# \"FailCode=E330\" -> [\"E330\"]\n# \"E101 and E207\" -> [\"E101\", \"E207\"]\n```\n\n### Keywords (Multi-language)\n\n```python\ndef find_keywords(text, keyword_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find which keywords from list appear in text\n    Case-insensitive, handles Chinese and English\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    found = []\n\n    for keyword in keyword_list:\n        keyword_lower = keyword.lower().strip()\n        if keyword_lower in text_lower:\n            found.append(keyword)\n\n    return found\n\n# Example:\n# keywords = [\"open\", \"开路\", \"short\", \"短路\"]\n# find_keywords(\"R152 开路，疑似虚焊\", keywords)\n# -> [\"开路\"]\n```\n\n## Advanced Patterns\n\n### Extract with Context\n```python\ndef extract_with_context(text, pattern, context_chars=20):\n    \"\"\"Extract matches with surrounding context\"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n        start = max(0, match.start() - context_chars)\n        end = min(len(text), match.end() + context_chars)\n        context = text[start:end]\n        matches.append({\n            'match': match.group(),\n            'context': context,\n            'start': match.start(),\n            'end': match.end()\n        })\n    return matches\n```\n\n### Non-Greedy Matching\n```python\n# Greedy (matches as much as possible)\nre.findall(r'<.*>', '<a>test</a>')  # ['<a>test</a>']\n\n# Non-greedy (matches as little as possible)\nre.findall(r'<.*?>', '<a>test</a>')  # ['<a>', '</a>']\n```\n\n### Lookahead and Lookbehind\n```python\n# Positive lookahead: match X only if followed by Y\npattern = r'\\b\\w+(?=\\s+open)'\nre.findall(pattern, \"R152 open, U202 short\")  # ['R152']\n\n# Negative lookahead: match X only if NOT followed by Y\npattern = r'\\b\\w+(?!\\s+open)'\n\n# Positive lookbehind: match X only if preceded by Y\npattern = r'(?<=suspect\\s)\\w+'\nre.findall(pattern, \"suspect cold solder\")  # ['cold']\n```\n\n## Flags\n\n```python\n# Case-insensitive\nre.findall(r'open', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n\n# Multiline mode (^ and $ match line boundaries)\nre.findall(r'^pattern', text, re.MULTILINE)\n\n# Dot matches newline\nre.findall(r'start.*end', text, re.DOTALL)\n\n# Verbose mode (allows comments in regex)\npattern = r\"\"\"\n    \\b              # Word boundary\n    ([A-Z]+\\d+)     # Component name\n    \\b              # Word boundary\n\"\"\"\nre.findall(pattern, text, re.VERBOSE)\n```\n\n## Common Manufacturing Patterns\n\n### Open Circuit Detection\n```python\ndef detect_open_circuit(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect open circuit mentions\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b(open)\\b',\n        r'\\b(开路)\\b',\n        r'\\bOS\\b',  # \"OS (open seen)\"\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n            return True\n    return False\n```\n\n### Short Circuit Detection\n```python\ndef detect_short_circuit(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect short circuit  return True\n    return False\n```\n\n### Fixture Contact Issues\n```python\ndef detect_fixture_contact(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect fixture/probe contact issues\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b(fixture.*contact)\\b',\n        r'\\b(probe.*worn)\\b',\n        r'\\b(pogo.*pin)\\b',\n        r'\\b(治具.*接触|探针.*接触)\\b',\n        r'\\b(重插.*OK|reseat.*pass)\\b',\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in extract_component_net_pairs(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract component and net name pairs\n    Example: \"R152_NET open\" -> component: R152, net: R152_NET\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern: Component name followed by underscore and net suffix\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)_([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+)\\b'\n\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n\n    results = []\n    for comp, net_suffix in `r'pattern'` to avoid escaping issues\n2. **Be specific** - Use `\\b` word boundaries to avoid partial matches\n3. **Case handling** - Use `re.IGNORECASE` or convert to lower/upper before matching\n4. - `\\b` prevents partial matches\n3. **Not escaping special chars** - `.` `*` `+` `?` etc. need escaping for literal match\n4. **Case sensitivity** - Remember to use `re.IGNORECASE` or normalize This is essential for normalizing manufacturing defect logs where engineers use informal language.\n\n## Key Libraries\n\n### 1. RapidFuzz (Recommended)\nFast string matching library with multiple algorithms.\n\n**Installation:**\n```bash\npip install rapidfuzz\n```\n\n**Key Functions:**\n\n#### `fuzz.ratio()` - Simple similarity (0-100)\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"fixture contact\", \"fixture 接触不良\")\n# Returns: similarity score 0-100\n```\n\n#### `fuzz.partial_ratio()` - Substring matching\n```python\nscore = fuzz.partial_ratio(\"open\", \"R152_NET open, suspect cold solder\")\n# Good for finding keywords in longer text\n```\n\n#### `fuzz.token_sort_ratio()` - Order-insensitive\n```python\nscore = fuzz.token_sort_ratio(\"cold solder U202\", \"U202 虚焊\")\n# Matches regardless of word order\n```\n\n#### `process.extractOne()` - Find best match from list\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import process, fuzz\n\nquery = \"治具/探针接触不良\"\nchoices = [\"Fixture contact unstable\", \"Cold solder suspected\", \"Open circuit\"]\nresult = process.extractOne(query, choices, scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio)\n# Returns: (best_match, score, index)\n```\n\n#### `process.extract()` - Find top N matches\n```python\nresults = process.extract(query, choices, limit=3, scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio)\n# Returns: [(match, score, index), ...]\n```\n\n### 2. difflib (Standard Library)\nPython's built-in string matching module.\n\n```python\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\ndef similarity(a, b):\n    return SequenceMatcher(None, a, b).ratio()\n\nscore = similarity(\"open circuit\", \"开路\")\n```\n\n## Common Patterns for Manufacturing Defect Matching\n\n### Pattern 1: Keyword-based matching\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\ndef match_by_keywords(text, codebook_keywords):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match text against keyword examples from codebook\n    codebook_keywords: \"open, 开路, C87, VDD_5V\"\n    \"\"\"\n    keywords = [k.strip() for k in codebook_keywords.split(',')]\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n\n    matches = []\n    for keyword in keywords:\n        if keyword.lower() in text_lower:\n            matches.append(keyword)\n\n    return len(matches) > 0, matches\n```\n\n### Pattern 2: Component extraction + text.upper())\n\ndef extract_net_names(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract net names like VDD_5V, SW_NODE\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b'\n    candidates = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n    # Filter out common abbreviations\n    return [c for c in candidates if len(c) > 2 and '_' in c]\n```\n\n### Pattern 3: Multi-language matching\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\n\ndef multilingual_match(text, standard_label, keywords_examples):\n    \"\"\"\n    Match considering both English and Chinese content\n    \"\"\"\n    # Combine label and keywords for comprehensive matching\n    reference_text = f\"{standard_label} {keywords_examples}\"\n\n    # Use token-based matching (word order independent)\n    score = fuzz.token_sort_ratio(text.lower(), reference_text.lower())\n\n    return score\n```\n\n### Pattern 4: Station-aware filtering\n```python\ndef filter_by_station(codebook_df, station):\n    \"\"\"\n    Filter codebook entries applicable to the test station\n    \"\"\"\n    mask = codebook_df['station_scope'].str.contains(station, case=False, na=False)\n    return codebook_df[mask]\n```\n\n## Complete Matching Pipeline\n\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz, process\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef find_best_match(raw_text, codebook_df, station, threshold=60):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching code from codebook\n\n    Parameters:\n    - raw_text: Engineer's raw input text\n    - codebook_df: DataFrame with columns [code, standard_label, keywords_examples, station_scope]\n    - station: Test station (ICT, FCT, etc.)\n    - threshold: Minimum confidence score (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n    - (code, label, confidence) or (None, None, 0)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter by station\n    station_filtered = filter_by_station(codebook_df, station)\n\n    if station_filtered.empty:\n        return None, None, 0.0\n\n    # Prepare search corpus (combine label + keywords)\n    search_corpus = (station_filtered['standard_label'] + ' ' +\n                     station_filtered['keywords_examples']).tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        raw_text,\n        search_corpus,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n    )\n\n    if result and result[1] >= threshold:\n        match_text, score, idx = result\n        matched_row = station_filtered.iloc[idx]\n        confidence = score / 100.0  # Normalize to 0-1\n        return matched_row['code'], matched_row['standard_label'], confidence\n\n    return None, None, 0.0\n```\n\n## Confidence Calibration\n\nFor engineering use, calibrate confidence scores:\n\n```python\ndef calibrate_confidence(raw_score, match_quality_factors):\n    \"\"\"\n    Adjust confidence based on match quality\n\n    raw_score: 0-100 from fuzzy matcher\n    match_quality_factors: dict with boolean flags\n    \"\"\"\n    confidence = raw_score / 100.0\n\n    # Boost confidence if exact component match\n    if match_quality_factors.get('component_match'):\n        confidence = min(1.0, confidence + 0.1)\n\n    # Boost if station matches\n    if match_quality_factors.get('station_match'):\n        confidence = min(1.0, confidence + 0.05)\n\n    # Reduce if no keyword overlap\n    if not match_quality_factors.get('keyword_overlap'):\n        confidence *= 0.8\n\n    return round(confidence, 2)\n```\n\n## Setting UNKNOWN threshold\n\n```python\nUNKNOWN_THRESHOLD = 0.6  # Below 60% confidence -> UNKNOWN\n\ndef normalize_with_unknown(text, codebook_df, station):\n    code, label, confidence = find_best_match(text, codebook_df, station, threshold=50)\n\n    if confidence < UNKNOWN_THRESHOLD:\n        return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", max(0.0, confidence - 0.2)  # Lower confidence for UNKNOWN\n\n    return code, label, confidence\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Always preprocess**: Lowercase, strip whitespace\n2. **Use token-based scorers**: Better for word order variations\n3. **Filter by context**: Station, product type before matching\n4. **Combine methods**: Keyword + fuzzy matching\n5. **Handle multilingual**: Chinese-English mixed text\n6. **Set appropriate thresholds**: Too low = This guide covers Python's json module and best practices for generating structured manufacturing data.\n\n## Basic JSON Operations\n\n### Import\n```python\nimport json\n```\n\n### Writing JSON\n\n#### Basic write to file\n```python\ndata = {\"name\": \"value\", \"items\": [1, 2, 3]}\n\n# Write to file\nwith open('output.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n```\n\n#### Convert to JSON string\n```python\njson_string = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n```\n\n### Reading JSON\n\n```python\n# Read from file\nwith open('input.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# Parse from string\ndata = json.loads(json_string)\n```\n\n## Important Parameters\n\n### `ensure_ascii=False`\n**Critical for Chinese/Unicode characters**\n```python\n# Without ensure_ascii=False\njson.dumps({\"text\": \"开路\"})\n# Output: '{\"text\": \"\\\\u5f00\\\\u8def\"}'\n\n# With ensure_ascii=False\njson.dumps({\"text\": \"开路\"}, ensure_ascii=False)\n# Output: '{\"text\": \"开路\"}'\n```\n\n### `indent`\n**For readable output**\n```python\n# Compact (no indent)\njson.dumps(data)\n# '{\"name\":\"value\",\"items\":[1,2,3]}'\n\n# Pretty printed (indent=2)\njson.dumps(data, indent=2)\n# {\n#   \"name\": \"value\",\n#   \"items\": [\n#     1,\n#     2,\n#     3\n#   ]\n# }\n```\n\n## Building Nested Structures\n\n### Manufacturing Test Record Format\n\n```python\ndef create_segment(segment_id, span_text, pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale):\n    \"\"\"Create a normalized segment\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n        \"span_text\": span_text,\n        \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n        \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n        \"confidence\": round(confidence, 2),  # Round to 2 decimals\n        \"rationale\": rationale\n    }\n\ndef create_record(record_id, product_id, station, engineer_id, raw_reason_text, normalized_segments):\n    \"\"\"Create a complete record\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"record_id\": record_id,\n        \"product_id\": product_id,\n        \"station\": station,\n        \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n        \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n        \"normalized\": normalized_segments\n    }\n\ndef create_solution(records):\n    \"\"\"Create final solution structure\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"records\": records\n    }\n```\n\n### Usage Example\n\n```python\n# Build segments\nsegments = []\nsegments.append(create_segment(\n    segment_id=\"R0000001-S1\",\n    span_text=\"治具/探针接触不良\",\n    pred_code=\"P3_RF-TS-019\",\n    pred_label=\"Fixture contact unstable (pogo pin / clamp)\",\n    confidence=0.92,\n    rationale=\"High keyword match: '治具', '探针', '接触不良' found in keywords_examples\"\n))\n\n# Build record\nrecord = create_record(\n    record_id=\"R0000001\",\n    product_id=\"P3_RF\",\n    station=\"ICT\",\n    engineer_id=\"E1\",\n    raw_reason_text=\"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK 浮高\",\n    normalized_segments=segments\n)\n\n# Build solution\nsolution = create_solution([record])\n\n# Write to file\nwith open('/app/output/solution.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n    json.dump(solution, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n```\n\n## Handling Special Data Types\n\n### Floats with precision\n```python\n# Round floats before JSON serialization\nconfidence = 0.8765432\ndata = {\"confidence\": round(confidence, 2)}  # 0.88\n```\n\n### None/null values\n```python\n# None becomes null in JSON\ndata = {\"value\": None}\njson.dumps(data)  # '{\"value\": null}'\n```\n\n### Empty strings\n```python\n# Empty strings are valid\ndata = {\"label\": \"\"}\njson.dumps(data)  # '{\"label\": \"\"}'\n```\n\n## Validation\n\n### Check JSON structure\n```python\ndef validate_solution(solution):\n    \"\"\"Validate solution structure\"\"\"\n    assert \"records\" in solution\n    assert isinstance(solution[\"records\"], list)\n\n    for record in solution[\"records\"]:\n        # Check required fields\n        required = [\"record_id\", \"product_id\", \"station\", \"engineer_id\", \"raw_reason_text\", \"normalized\"]\n        for field in required:\n            assert field in record, f\"Missing field: {field}\"\n\n        # Check normalized segments\n        assert isinstance(record[\"normalized\"], list)\n        for segment in record[\"normalized\"]:\n            required_seg = [\"segment_id\", \"span_text\", \"pred_code\", \"pred_label\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\"]\n            for field in required_seg:\n                assert field in segment, f\"Missing segment field: {field}\"\n\n            # Check confidence range\n            assert 0.0 <= segment[\"confidence\"] <= 1.0\n```\n\n### Pretty print for debugging\n```python\ndef print_json(data):\n    \"\"\"Print JSON in readable format\"\"\"\n    print(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))\n```\n\n## Building from DataFrames\n\n### Convert DataFrame rows to JSON records\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\n\ndef dataframe_to_records(df):\n    \"\"\"Convert DataFrame to list of record dictionaries\"\"\"\n    records = []\n\n    for idx, row in df.iterrows():\n        record = {\n            \"record_id\": row['record_id'],\n            \"product_id\": row['product_id'],\n            \"station\": row['station'],\n            # Proper segmentation is critical before matching each segment to codebook entries.\n\n## Common Delimiters in Manufacturing Logs\n\n### Explicit Delimiters\n- `;` - Semicolon\n- `、` - Chinese enumeration comma\n- `&` - Ampersand\n- `+` - Plus sign\n- `/` - Forward slash (sometimes)\n- `|` - Pipe\n\n### Connective Phrases\n- `and` / `且` / `并且`\n- `also` / `另` / `还有`\n- `or` / `或` / `或者`\n\n### Example Texts with Multiple Issues\n```\n\"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK 浮高 & 静态电流偏高，疑似短路趋势/器件异常\"\n\n\"SPI_MISO open, suspect cold solder + probe worn causing bad contact, replace pin\"\n\n\"I2C_SDA 开路，疑似虚焊/少锡；Station=ICT, FailCode=E330，建议复测确认\"\n```\n\n## Segmentation Strategies\n\n### Strategy 1: Simple Delimiter Splitting\n\n```python\ndef simple_segment(text):\n    \"\"\"Split by common delimiters\"\"\"\n    delimiters = [';', '、', '&', '+']\n\n    segments = [text]\n    for delimiter in delimiters:\n        new_segments = []\n        for seg in segments:\n            new_segments.extend(seg.split(delimiter))\n        segments = new_segments\n\n    # Clean and filter\n    segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n    return segments\n```\n\n### Strategy 2: Multi-Character Delimiter Splitting\n\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef regex_segment(text):\n    \"\"\"Split by multiple delimiters using regex\"\"\"\n    # Split by ; & + 、 and connective phrases\n    pattern = r'[;;&+、]|(?:\\s+and\\s+)|(?:\\s+或\\s+)|(?:\\s+并且\\s+)'\n    segments = re.split(pattern, text)\n\n    # Clean whitespace and empty strings\n    segments = [s.strip() for s in segments if s.strip()]\n    return segments\n```\n\n### Strategy 3: Contextual Segmentation\n\n```python\ndef contextual_segment(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Segment based on manufacturing context patterns\n    Looks for issue indicators that signal new segments\n    \"\"\"\n    # Indicators of new issues\n    indicators = [\n        r'(?:^|\\s)(?:suspect|疑似|可能)',  # Suspicion words\n        r'(?:^|\\s)(?:also|另|还有|并且)',   # Additional issue words\n        r'(?:^|\\s)(?:and|且)',              # Conjunction\n    ]\n\n    # Try to split at indicators\n    for pattern in indicators:\n        if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n            # More sophisticated splitting logic here\n            pass\n\n    # Fallback to simple delimiter split\n    return regex_segment(text)\n```\n\n### Strategy 4: Slash Handling (Special Case)\n\nSlashes can mean \"or\" (multiple descriptions of same issue) or separate issues:\n\n```python\ndef smart_slash_handling(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Handle slashes intelligently\n    - \"虚焊/浮高\" = cold solder OR floating (same issue, synonyms)\n    - \"issue1 / issue2\" = separate issues if contextually different\n    \"\"\"\n    # Split by major delimiters first\n    major_segments = regex_segment(text)\n\n    final_segments = []\n    for seg in major_segments:\n        # Check if slash separates synonyms or separate issues\n        if '/' in seg:\n            parts = seg.split('/')\n\n            # If parts are short and similar (likely synonyms), keep together\n            if len(parts) == 2 and len(parts[0]) < 20 and len(parts[1]) < 20:\n                # Keep as one segment (synonyms)\n                final_segments.append(seg)\n            else:\n                # Split as separate issues\n                final_segments.extend([p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()])\n        else:\n            final_segments.append(seg)\n\n    return final_segments\n```\n\n## Handling Nested Information\n\nSome texts contain issue + context (not separate issues):\n\n```python\ndef extract_primary_issues(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract primary issues while preserving context\n\n    Example:\n    \"I2C_SDA 开路，疑似虚焊/少锡；Station=ICT\"\n    -> Primary issue: \"I2C_SDA 开路，疑似虚焊/少锡\"\n    -> Metadata: \"Station=ICT\" (keep with main issue)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove metadata patterns that aren't actual issues\n    metadata_patterns = [\n        r'Station\\s*=\\s*\\w+',\n        r'FailCode\\s*=\\s*\\w+',\n        r'建议\\w+确认',\n        r'需确认\\w*',\n    ]\n\n    cleaned = text\n    for pattern in metadata_patterns:\n        cleaned = re.sub(pattern, '', cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    # Now segment the cleaned text\n    return regex_segment(cleaned)\n```\n\n## Complete Segmentation Pipeline\n\n```python\nimport re\n\nclass TextSegmenter:\n    \"\"\"Complete text segmentation for manufacturing defect reports\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.major_delimiters = r'[;;&、]'\n        self.minor_delimiters = r'[+]'\n        self.connectives = r'(?:\\s+and\\s+)|(?:\\s+或\\s+)|(?:\\s+并且\\s+)|(?:\\s+also\\s+)'\n\n    def segment(self, text):\n        \"\"\"Main segmentation method\"\"\"\n        if not text or not text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Remove metadata\n        text = self._remove_metadata(text)\n\n        # Split by major delimiters\n        segments = self._split_by_delimiters(text)\n\n        # Clean and validate\n        segments = self._clean_segments(segments)\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _remove_metadata(self, text):\n        \"\"\"Remove non-issue metadata\"\"\"\n        patterns = [\n            r'Station\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n            r'FailCode\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n        ]\n\n        cleaned = text\n        for pattern in patterns:\n            cleaned = re.sub(pattern, '', cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        return cleaned.strip()\n\n    def _split_by_delimiters(self, text):\n        \"\"\"Split by all delimiter types\"\"\"\n        # Combine all delimiter patterns\n        pattern = f'({self.major_delimiters}|{self.connectives})'\n        segments = re.split(pattern, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        # Filter out the delimiter matches themselves\n        segments = [s for s in segments if not re.match(pattern, s, re.IGNORECASE)]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _clean_segments(self, segments):\n        \"\"\"Clean, filter and deduplicate segments\"\"\"\n        cleaned = []\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Strip whitespace\n            seg = seg.strip()\n            seg = seg.strip(',，.。')  # Remove trailing punctuation\n\n            # Skip empty or too short\n            if not seg or len(seg) < 3:\n                continue\n\n            # Skip pure punctuation or numbers\n            if re.match(r'^[\\W\\d]+$', seg):\n                continue\n\n            cleaned.append(seg)\n\n        # Remove duplicates while preserving order\n        seen = set()\n        unique = []\n        for seg in cleaned:\n            if seg.lower() not in seen:\n                seen.add(seg.lower())\n                unique.append(seg)\n\n        return unique\n\n# Usage\nsegmenter = TextSegmenter()\nsegments = segmenter.segment(\"治具/探针接触不良 & 静态电流偏高，疑似短路\")\n# Returns: [\"治具/探针接触不良\", \"静态电流偏高，疑似短路\"]\n```\n\n## Creating Segment IDs\n\n```python\ndef create_segment_id(record_id, segment_index):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create segment ID in format: <record_id>-S<i>\n\n    Example: \"R0000001\" + 1 -> \"R0000001-S1\"\n    \"\"\"\n    return f\"{record_id}-S{segment_index}\"\n\n# Usage in processing\ndef process_record(record_id, raw_text):\n    segmenter = TextSegmenter()\n    segments = segmenter.segment(raw_text)\n\n    segment_results = []\n    for i, seg_text in enumerate(segments, start=1):\n        segment_id = create_segment_id(record_id, i)\n\n        # Process this segment...\n        result = {\n            \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n            \"span_text\": seg_text,\n            # ... other fields\n        }\n        segment_results.append(result)\n\n    return segment_results\n```\n\n## Edge Cases\n\n### Single Issue (No Segmentation Needed)\n```python\ntext = \"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK\"\nsegments = segmenter.segment(text)\n# Returns: [\"治具/探针接触不良，重插后偶尔OK\"]  (one segment)\n```\n\n### Empty or Invalid Text\n```python\ntext = \"\"\nsegments = segmenter.segment(text)\n# Returns: []\n\ntext = \"   \"\nsegments = segmenter.segment(text)\n# Returns: []\n```\n\n### Complex Multi-Language\n```python\ntext = \"idle current high, suspect partial short/component issue 重测过 & fixture contact unstable, reseat sometimes passes\"\nsegments = segmenter.segment(text)\n# Returns: [\n#   \"idle current high, suspect partial short/component issue 重测过\",\n#   \"fixture contact unstable, reseat sometimes passes\"\n# ]\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Preserve context** - Keep descriptive parts with main issue\n2. **Handle synonyms** - \"虚焊/浮高\" should stay together\n3. **Remove metadata** - Filter out non-issue annotations\n4. **Clean thoroughly** - Remove extra whitespace, punctuation\n5. **Validate segments** - Ensure each segment is meaningful (not too short)\n6. **Test with real data** - Manufacturing text is messy, test edge cases\n7. **Use segment IDs** - Track which segment came from which record\n\n## Testing Segmentation\n\n```python\ndef test_segmentation():\n    segmenter = TextSegmenter()\n\n    test_cases = [\n        (\"治具/探针接触不良 & 静态电流偏高\", 2),\n        (\"SPI_MISO open, suspect cold solder\", 1),\n        (\"issue1; issue2; issue3\", 3),\n        (\"\", 0),\n    ]\n\n    for text, expected_count in test_cases:\n        segments = segmenter.segment(text)\n        assert len(segments) == expected_count, f\"Failed: {text}\"\n\n    print(\"All segmentation tests Regular Expression Pattern Matching\n\n## Overview\nRegular expressions (regex) are essential for extracting structured information from unstructured manufacturing defect text, such as component names, net names, and keywords.\n\n## Python re Module\n\n```python\nimport re\n```\n\n## Basic Pattern Matching\n\n### Search for pattern\n```python\n# Find first match\nmatch = re.search(r'pattern', text)\nif match:\n    found_text = match.group()\n\n# Find all matches\nmatches = re.findall(r'pattern', text)\n# Returns: list of matched strings\n```\n\n### Match at start\n```python\n# Check if text starts with pattern\nmatch = re.match(r'^pattern', text)\n```\n\n### Replace patterns\n```python\n# Replace all occurrences\nnew_text = re.sub(r'pattern', 'replacement', text)\n\n# Replace with function\ndef replacer(match):\n    return match.group().upper()\n\nnew_text = re.sub(r'pattern', replacer, text)\n```\n\n### Split by pattern\n```python\n# Split text by delimiter pattern\nparts = re.split(r'[;,&]', text)\n```\n\n## Common Patterns for Manufacturing Data\n\n### Component Names\n```python\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract component references: U202, R152, C87, Q15, etc.\n    Format: Letter(s) + Number(s)\n    \"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b'\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n    return matches\n\n# Examples:\n# \"U202 虚焊\" -> [\"U202\"]\n# \"R152_NET open\" -> [\"R152\"]\n# \"C87 and Q15\" -> [\"C87\", \"Q15\"]\n```\n\n### Net Names / Signal Names\n```python\ndef extract_net_names(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract net/signal names: VDD_5V, SW_NODE, I2C_SDA, etc.\n    Format: CAPS with underscores\n    \"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,})\\b'\n    candidates = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n\n    # Filter out common abbreviations that aren't net names\n    exclusions = {'OK', 'NG', 'ICT', 'FCT', 'BST', 'ESD'}\n    net_names = [c for c in candidates if c not in exclusions and '_' in c]\n\n    return net_names\n\n# Examples:\n# \"SW_NODE open\" -> [\"SW_NODE\"]\n# \"I2C_SDA 开路\" -> [\"I2C_SDA\"]\n# \"VDD_5V and GND\" -> [\"VDD_5V\", \"GND\"]\n```\n\n### Test Station Names\n```python\ndef extract_station(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract test station names\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b(ICT|FCT|BST|FUNC|FLASH|BURNIN|HIPOT|RF_CAL)\\b'\n    match = re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    return match.group().upper() if match else None\n\n# Examples:\n# \"Station=ICT\" -> \"ICT\"\n# \"at FCT station\" -> \"FCT\"\n```\n\n### Error/Fail Codes\n```python\ndef extract_fail_codes(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract fail codes: E101, E702, F001, S021, B090, etc.\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]\\d{3,4})\\b'\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n    return matches\n\n# Examples:\n# \"FailCode=E330\" -> [\"E330\"]\n# \"E101 and E207\" -> [\"E101\", \"E207\"]\n```\n\n### Keywords (Multi-language)\n\n```python\ndef find_keywords(text, keyword_list):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find which keywords from list appear in text\n    Case-insensitive, handles Chinese and English\n    \"\"\"\n    text_lower = text.lower()\n    found = []\n\n    for keyword in keyword_list:\n        keyword_lower = keyword.lower().strip()\n        if keyword_lower in text_lower:\n            found.append(keyword)\n\n    return found\n\n# Example:\n# keywords = [\"open\", \"开路\", \"short\", \"短路\"]\n# find_keywords(\"R152 开路，疑似虚焊\", keywords)\n# -> [\"开路\"]\n```\n\n## Advanced Patterns\n\n### Extract with Context\n```python\ndef extract_with_context(text, pattern, context_chars=20):\n    \"\"\"Extract matches with surrounding context\"\"\"\n    matches = []\n    for match in re.finditer(pattern, text):\n        start = max(0, match.start() - context_chars)\n        end = min(len(text), match.end() + context_chars)\n        context = text[start:end]\n        matches.append({\n            'match': match.group(),\n            'context': context,\n            'start': match.start(),\n            'end': match.end()\n        })\n    return matches\n```\n\n### Non-Greedy Matching\n```python\n# Greedy (matches as much as possible)\nre.findall(r'<.*>', '<a>test</a>')  # ['<a>test</a>']\n\n# Non-greedy (matches as little as possible)\nre.findall(r'<.*?>', '<a>test</a>')  # ['<a>', '</a>']\n```\n\n### Lookahead and Lookbehind\n```python\n# Positive lookahead: match X only if followed by Y\npattern = r'\\b\\w+(?=\\s+open)'\nre.findall(pattern, \"R152 open, U202 short\")  # ['R152']\n\n# Negative lookahead: match X only if NOT followed by Y\npattern = r'\\b\\w+(?!\\s+open)'\n\n# Positive lookbehind: match X only if preceded by Y\npattern = r'(?<=suspect\\s)\\w+'\nre.findall(pattern, \"suspect cold solder\")  # ['cold']\n```\n\n## Flags\n\n```python\n# Case-insensitive\nre.findall(r'open', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n\n# Multiline mode (^ and $ match line boundaries)\nre.findall(r'^pattern', text, re.MULTILINE)\n\n# Dot matches newline\nre.findall(r'start.*end', text, re.DOTALL)\n\n# Verbose mode (allows comments in regex)\npattern = r\"\"\"\n    \\b              # Word boundary\n    ([A-Z]+\\d+)     # Component name\n    \\b              # Word boundary\n\"\"\"\nre.findall(pattern, text, re.VERBOSE)\n```\n\n## Common Manufacturing Patterns\n\n### Open Circuit Detection\n```python\ndef detect_open_circuit(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect open circuit mentions\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b(open)\\b',\n        r'\\b(开路)\\b',\n        r'\\bOS\\b',  # \"OS (open seen)\"\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n            return True\n    return False\n```\n\n### Short Circuit Detection\n```python\ndef detect_short_circuit(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect short circuit mentions\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b(short)\\b',\n        r'\\b(短路)\\b',\n        r'\\b(bridge|桥连|连锡)\\b',\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n            return True\n    return False\n```\n\n### Cold Solder Detection\n```python\ndef detect_cold_solder(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect cold solder mentions\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b(cold\\s+solder)\\b',\n        r'\\b(虚焊|浮高|少锡)\\b',\n        r'\\b(intermittent)\\b',\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n            return True\n    return False\n```\n\n### Fixture Contact Issues\n```python\ndef detect_fixture_contact(text):\n    \"\"\"Detect fixture/probe contact issues\"\"\"\n    patterns = [\n        r'\\b(fixture.*contact)\\b',\n        r'\\b(probe.*worn)\\b',\n        r'\\b(pogo.*pin)\\b',\n        r'\\b(治具.*接触|探针.*接触)\\b',\n        r'\\b(重插.*OK|reseat.*pass)\\b',\n    ]\n\n    for pattern in patterns:\n        if re.search(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):\n            return True\n    return False\n```\n\n## Component + Net Extraction Combined\n\n```python\ndef extract_component_net_pairs(text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract component and net name pairs\n    Example: \"R152_NET open\" -> component: R152, net: R152_NET\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern: Component name followed by underscore and net suffix\n    pattern = r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)_([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+)\\b'\n\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, text.upper())\n\n    results = []\n    for comp, net_suffix in matches:\n        full_net = f\"{comp}_{net_suffix}\"\n        results.append({\n            'component': comp,\n            'net': full_net\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n# Example:\n# \"R152_NET open, U12_VDD short\"\n# -> [\n#      {'component': 'R152', 'net': 'R152_NET'},\n#      {'component': 'U12', 'net': 'U12_VDD'}\n#    ]\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Use raw strings** - `r'pattern'` to avoid escaping issues\n2. **Be specific** - Use `\\b` word boundaries to avoid partial matches\n3. **Case handling** - Use `re.IGNORECASE` or convert to lower/upper before matching\n4. **Test patterns** - Use online regex testers (regex101.com) with sample data\n5. **Escape special chars** - Use `re.escape()` for literal matching\n6. **Compile for reuse** - `pattern = re.compile(r'...')` for better performance\n7. **Handle Unicode** - Python 3 handles Unicode by default in regex\n\n## Testing Regex Patterns\n\n```python\ndef test_component_extraction():\n    \"\"\"Test component extraction patterns\"\"\"\n    test_cases = [\n        (\"U202 虚焊\", [\"U202\"]),\n        (\"R152_NET open\", [\"R152\"]),\n        (\"C87 and Q15 short\", [\"C87\", \"Q15\"]),\n        (\"no components here\", []),\n    ]\n\n    for text, expected in test_cases:\n        result = extract_components(text)\n        assert result == expected, f\"Failed for: {text}, got {result}\"\n\n    print(\"All tests passed!\")\n\ntest_component_extraction()\n```\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n1. **Greedy matching** - Use `*?` or `+?` for non-greedy\n2. **Forgetting word boundaries** - `\\b` prevents partial matches\n3. **Not escaping special chars** - `.` `*` `+` `?` etc. need escaping for literal match\n4. **Case sensitivity** - Remember to use `re.IGNORECASE` or normalize case\n5. **Unicode issues** - Ensure proper encoding when working with Chinese text\n6. **Group capturing** - Use `()` to capture parts, `(?:)` for non-capturing groups\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

individual issues\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.major_delimiters = r'[;;&、]'\n        self.connectives = r'(?:\\s+and\\s+)|(?:\\s+或\\s+)|(?:\\s+并且\\s+)|(?:\\s+also\\s+)'\n\n    def segment(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Segment text into individual issues\"\"\"\n        if not text or not text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Remove metadata that's not part of actual issues\n        text = self._remove_metadata(text)\n\n        # Split by delimiters\n        segments = self._split_by_delimiters(text)\n\n        # Clean and validate\n        segments = self._clean_segments(segments)\n\n        # If no segments found, return original text as single segment\n        if not segments and text.strip():\n            return [text.strip()]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _remove_metadata(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Remove non-issue metadata\"\"\"\n        patterns = [\n            r'Station\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n            r'FailCode\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n        ]\n\n        cleaned = text\n        for pattern in patterns:\n            cleaned = re.sub(pattern, '', cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        return f'({self.major_delimiters}|{self.connectives})'\n        segments = re.split(pattern, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        # Filter out the delimiter matches themselves\n        segments = [s for s in segments if not re.match(pattern, s, re.IGNORECASE)]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _clean_segments(self, segments: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Clean, filter and deduplicate         # Skip empty or too short\n            if not seg or len(seg) < 3:\n                continue\n\n            # Skip pure punctuation or numbers\n            if re.match(r'^[\\W\\d]+$', seg):\n                continue\n\n            cleaned.append(seg)\n\n        # Remove duplicates while preserving order\n        seen = set()\n        unique = []\n        for seg in cleaned:\n            seg_normalized = seg.lower().strip()\n            if seg_normalized not in seen:\n                seen.add(seg_normalized)\n                unique.append(seg)\n\n        return unique\n\n\nclass self._prepare_codebook()\n\n    def _prepare_codebook(self):\n        \"\"\"Prepare codebook for efficient matching\"\"\"\n        # Create combined search text (label + keywords)\n Optional\n\n\nclass TextSegmenter:\n    \"\"\"Segments multi-issue defect text into individual issues\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.major_delimiters = r'[;;&、]'\n        self.connectives = r'(?:\\s+and\\s+)|(?:\\s+或\\s+)|(?:\\s+并且\\s+)|(?:\\s+also\\s+)'\n\n    def segment(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Segment text into individual issues\"\"\"\n        if not text or not text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Remove metadata that's not part of actual issues\n        text = self._remove_metadata(text)\n\n        # Split by delimiters\n        segments = self._split_by_delimiters(text)\n\n        # Clean and validate\n        segments = self._clean_segments(segments)\n\n        # If no segments found, return original text as single segment\n        if not segments and text.strip():\n            return [text.strip()]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _remove_metadata(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Remove non-issue metadata\"\"\"\n        patterns = [\n            r'Station\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n            r'FailCode\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n        ]\n\n        cleaned = text\n        for pattern in patterns:\n            cleaned = re.sub(pattern, '', cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        return cleaned.strip()\n\n    def _split_by_delimiters(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Split by all delimiter types\"\"\"\n        # Combine delimiter patterns\n        pattern = f'({self.major_delimiters}|{self.connectives})'\n        segments = re.split(pattern, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        # Filter out the delimiter matches themselves\n        segments = [s for s in segments if not re.match(pattern, s, re.IGNORECASE)]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _clean_segments(self, segments: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Clean, filter and deduplicate segments\"\"\"\n        cleaned = []\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Strip whitespace and trailing punctuation\n            seg = seg.strip()\n            seg = seg.strip(',，.。')\n\n            # Skip empty or too short\n            if not seg or len(seg) < 3:\n                continue\n\n            # Skip pure punctuation or numbers\n            if re.match(r'^[\\W\\d]+$', seg):\n                continue\n\n            cleaned.append(seg)\n\n        # Remove duplicates while preserving order\n        seen = set()\n        unique = []\n        for seg in cleaned:\n            seg_normalized = seg.lower().strip()\n            if seg_normalized not in seen:\n                seen.add(seg_normalized)\n                unique.append(seg)\n\n        return unique\n\n\nclass DefectMatcher:\n    \"\"\"Matches defect text to standardized codebook entries\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, codebook_df: pd.DataFrame):\n        self.codebook_df = codebook_df\n        self._prepare_codebook()\n\n    def _prepare_codebook(self):\n        \"\"\"Prepare codebook for efficient matching\"\"\"\n        # Create combined search text (label + keywords)\n        self.codebook_df['search_text'] = (\n            self.codebook_df['standard_label'] + ' ' +\n            self.codebook_df['keywords_examples']\n        ).str.lower()\n\n    def match(self, text: str, station: str, threshold: float = 50.0) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Match text to best codebook entry\n\n        Returns:\n            (code, label, confidence, rationale) or (None, None, 0.0, reason)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Filter by station\n        station_filtered = self._filter_by_station(station)\n\n        if station_filtered.empty:\n            return None, None, 0.0, f\"No codebook entries for station '{station}'\"\n\n        # Extract components and nets from input text\n        components = self._extract_components(text)\n        net_names = self._extract_net_names(text)\n\n        # Prepare search corpus\n        search_corpus = station_filtered['search_text'].tolist()\n\n        # Find best fuzzy match\n        result = process.extractOne(\n            text.lower(),\n            search_corpus,\n            scorer=fuzz.token_sort_ratio\n        )\n\n        if not result:\n            return None, None, 0.0, \"No match found\"\n\n        match_text, score, idx = result\n        matched_row = station_filtered.iloc[idx]\n\n        # Calculate calibrated confidence\n        confidence, rationale = self._calibrate_confidence(\n            text=text,\n            raw_score=score,\n            matched_row=matched_row,\n            components=components,\n            net_names=net_names,\n            station=station\n        )\n\n        # If confidence too low, return UNKNOWN\n        if confidence < 0.5:\n            return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", max(0.0, confidence * 0.6), f\"Low match confidence ({confidence:.2f}): {rationale}\"\n\n        return matched_row['code'], \"\"\"Segments multi-issue defect text into individual issues\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.major_delimiters = r'[;;&、]'\n        self.connectives = text.strip():\n            return []\n\n        # Remove metadata that's not part of actual issues\n        text = self._remove_metadata(text)\n\n        # Split by delimiters\n        segments = self._split_by_delimiters(text)\n\n        # Clean and validate\n        segments = self._clean_segments(segments)\n\n        # If no segments found, return original text as single segment\n        if  patterns = [\n            r'Station\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n            r'FailCode\\s*=\\s*\\w+,?\\s*',\n        ]\n\n        cleaned = text\n        for pattern in patterns:\n            cleaned =      # Combine delimiter patterns\n        pattern = f'({self.major_delimiters}|{self.connectives})'\n        segments = re.split(pattern, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n        # Filter out the delimiter matches themselves\n        segments = [s for s in segments if not re.match(pattern, s, re.IGNORECASE)]\n\n        return segments\n\n    def _clean_segments(self, segments: List[str]) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"Clean, filter and deduplicate segments\"\"\"\n        cleaned = []\n\n        for seg in segments:\n            # Strip whitespace and trailing punctuation\n            seg = numbers\n            if re.match(r'^[\\W\\d]+$', seg):\n                continue\n\n            cleaned.append(seg)\n\n        # Remove duplicates while preserving order\n        seen = set()\n confidence < 0.5:\n            return \"UNKNOWN\", \"\", max(0.0, confidence * 0.6), f\"Low match confidence ({confidence:.2f}): {rationale}\"\n\n        return matched_row['code'], {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01Xskhb135EMABU3HmVQWxHC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_013kp9Ct9HN73pPp5GD5jdAg","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/README.md","content":"# Manufacturing Defect Text Normalization Solution\n\n## Overview\n\nThis solution normalizes hand-written manufacturing defect reason texts from test center logs into standardized codebook entries. Testing engineers often write defect reasons with typos, abbreviations, Chinese-English mixtures, and informal language. This system maps these noisy inputs to standardized defect codes for analysis and tracking.\n\n## Solution Files\n\n### Main Script\n- **`normalize_defects.py`** - Complete normalization system\n  - Text segmentation for multi-issue defects\n  - Fuzzy string matching with confidence calibration\n  - Product-specific codebook loading\n  - JSON output generation\n\n### Output\n- **`/app/output/solution.json`** - Normalized results (8.6 MB, 12,000 records)\n\n### Skill Documents (in `/app/environment/skills/`)\n1. **`fuzzy-string-matching.md`** - String matching techniques for noisy text\n2. **`pandas-csv-processing.md`** - CSV data processing with Pandas\n3. **`json-output-formatting.md`** - JSON structure creation and formatting\n4. **`text-segmentation.md`** - Multi-issue text segmentation strategies\n5. **`regex-pattern-matching.md`** - Pattern extraction for components/nets\n\n## How It Works\n\n### 1. Text Segmentation\nSplits compound defect descriptions into individual issues:\n- Input: `\"issue1 & issue2; issue3\"`\n- Output: `[\"issue1\", \"issue2\", \"issue3\"]`\n\n### 2. Fuzzy Matching\nMatches each segment to standardized codebook entries:\n- Uses RapidFuzz with token_sort_ratio (order-independent)\n- Filters by test station (ICT, FCT, BST, etc.)\n- Handles multilingual text (Chinese + English)\n\n### 3. Confidence Calibration\nAdjusts confidence based on match quality:\n- Base score from fuzzy matching (0-100)\n- Boosts for keyword matches, component matches, net name matches\n- Reduces for missing keyword overlap\n- Threshold: < 0.5 → UNKNOWN\n\n### 4. Output Generation\nCreates structured JSON with:\n- Segment IDs: `R0000001-S1`, `R0000001-S2`, etc.\n- Prediction codes from codebook or \"UNKNOWN\"\n- Confidence scores (0.0 - 1.0)\n- Detailed rationale for each prediction\n\n## Running the Solution\n\n```bash\n# Install dependencies\npip install rapidfuzz pandas\n\n# Run normalization\npython normalize_defects.py\n\n# Output will be at /app/output/solution.json\n```\n\n## Results\n\nProcessed **12,000 test log records** → **14,178 segments**\n\n### Performance\n- **Known predictions**: 6,481 (45.7%)\n- **Unknown predictions**: 7,697 (54.3%)\n\n### Confidence Statistics\n| Metric | All | Known | Unknown |\n|--------|-----|-------|---------|\n| Mean | 0.400 | 0.695 | 0.152 |\n| Median | 0.230 | 0.720 | 0.150 |\n\nThe clear separation between known (0.7) and unknown (0.15) confidence levels enables effective engineering review prioritization.\n\n## Example Results\n\n### Simple Match\n**Input:** `\"program/flash write failed, reflash unstable\"`\n- Code: `P2_CTRL-SW-025`\n- Label: `Programming failed (flash write)`\n- Confidence: `0.64`\n\n### Multi-Segment Match\n**Input:** `\"idle current high & fixture contact unstable\"`\n\nSegment 1:\n- Code: `P1_POWER-EL-037`\n- Label: `Idle current high / abnormal consumption`\n- Confidence: `0.58`\n\nSegment 2:\n- Code: `P1_POWER-TS-019`\n- Label: `Fixture contact unstable (pogo pin / clamp)`\n- Confidence: `0.75`\n\n### Unknown (Low Confidence)\n**Input:** `\"校验失败/CRC挂，疑似程序包或数据异常\"`\n- Code: `UNKNOWN`\n- Label: `` (empty)\n- Confidence: `0.06`\n- Rationale: `Low match confidence (0.10): Fuzzy match score: 11.8...`\n\n## Key Features\n\n✓ **Multilingual Support** - This system maps these noisy inputs to standardized defect codes for analysis and tracking.\n\n## Solution Files\n\n### Main Script\n- **`normalize_defects.py`** - Complete normalization system\n  - Text segmentation for multi-issue defects\n  - Fuzzy string matching with confidence calibration\n  - Product-specific codebook loading\n  - JSON output generation\n\n### Output\n- **`/app/output/solution.json`** - Normalized results (8.6 MB, 12,000 records)\n\n### Skill Documents (in `/app/environment/skills/`)\n1. **`fuzzy-string-matching.md`** - String matching techniques for noisy text\n2. **`pandas-csv-processing.md`** - CSV data processing with Pandas\n3. **`json-output-formatting.md`** - JSON structure creation and formatting\n4. **`text-segmentation.md`** - Multi-issue text segmentation strategies\n5. **`regex-pattern-matching.md`** - Pattern extraction for components/nets\n\n## How It Works\n\n### 1.
